          - list
            - listitem [level=1]
              - ListMarker "• "
              - StaticText "Fixed a bug when the DRADeviceTaintRules feature is enabled that caused kube-scheduler to panic when DeviceTaintRules exist and ResourceSlices are changed or to ignore new changes to DeviceTaintRules. ("
              - link "#139681" [ref=e607]
              - StaticText ", "
              - link "@nojnhuh" [ref=e608]
              - StaticText ") [SIG Node and Testing]"
          - generic
            - heading "Bug or Regression" [level=3, ref=e154]
            - link "Permalink: Bug or Regression" [ref=e155]
          - list
            - listitem [level=1]
              - ListMarker "• "
              - StaticText "Cri-api: Reverts to pre-1.34 JSON encoding of the KeyValue value field ("
              - link "#139965" [ref=e609]
              - StaticText ", "
              - link "@liggitt" [ref=e610]
              - StaticText ") [SIG Node]"
            - listitem [level=1]
              - ListMarker "• "
              - StaticText "Fixed DRA scheduling bugs where the structured allocator mis-counted a device's shared counters while exploring candidates: it could keep a counter reserved after rejecting or backtracking a candidate, or drop a shared device's in-use marker so a later share was charged the counter twice. Either way the allocator could treat a counter set as exhausted and leave a pod pending on a node that could satisfy it. This affected the allocator used by the default feature configuration. ("
              - link "#140663" [ref=e611]
              - StaticText ", "
              - link "@thc1006" [ref=e612]
              - StaticText ") [SIG Node]"
            - listitem [level=1]
              - ListMarker "• "
              - StaticText "Fixed a kubelet memory leak regression in 1.36 caused by leaked contexts on every Pod sync. ("
              - link "#140066" [ref=e613]
              - StaticText ", "
              - link "@compumike" [ref=e614]
              - StaticText ") [SIG Node]"
            - listitem [level=1]
              - ListMarker "• "
              - StaticText "Fixes a 1.36 regression in server side apply where patching a container type (list or map) could result in "
              - code
                - StaticText "422 required"
              - StaticText " errors for apply requests that previously succeeded. ("
              - link "#140296" [ref=e615]
              - StaticText ", "
              - link "@jpbetz" [ref=e616]
              - StaticText ") [SIG API Machinery, Architecture, Auth, CLI, Cloud Provider, Cluster Lifecycle, Network, Node, Scheduling and Storage]"
            - listitem [level=1]
              - ListMarker "• "
              - StaticText "Kubeadm: Improved resilience of kubeadm etcd learner promotion. kubeadm now correctly handles cases where learner promotion succeeds on the etcd side but a transient client-side error is returned, preventing unnecessary etcd-join failures. ("
              - link "#139910" [ref=e617]
              - StaticText ", "
              - link "@jihyun-huh" [ref=e618]
              - StaticText ") [SIG Cluster Lifecycle]"
            - listitem [level=1]
              - ListMarker "• "
              - StaticText "Kubeadm: during \"kubeadm join\", use the KubernetesAPICall timeout (default 1 minute) when fetching the kubeadm-config ConfigMap, instead of the short 350ms retry used for optional component configs. A new shortConfigMapGet parameter is added to FetchInitConfigurationFromCluster so that callers like \"kubeadm reset\" can still use the short retry. ("
              - link "#139808" [ref=e619]

              - StaticText "DRA metadata read helper (KEP-5304): on decode skip only if version is unknown, return error if object/file is malformed ("
              - link "#138859" [ref=e760]
              - StaticText ", "
              - link "@alaypatel07" [ref=e761]
              - StaticText ") [SIG Node]"
            - listitem [level=1]
              - ListMarker "• "
              - StaticText "Fixed kubelet failure starting on ZFS due to missing cadvisor plugin. ("
              - link "#138590" [ref=e762]
              - StaticText ", "
              - link "@BenTheElder" [ref=e763]
              - StaticText ") [SIG Node]"
            - listitem [level=1]
              - ListMarker "• "
              - StaticText "Fixed stale remote HNS endpoint cleanup on Windows when a pod IP is reused across nodes in L2Bridge networks, preventing DNS timeouts caused by traffic being routed to the wrong node. ("
              - link "#138603" [ref=e764]
              - StaticText ", "
              - link "@princepereira" [ref=e765]
              - StaticText ") [SIG Network and Windows]"
            - listitem [level=1]
              - ListMarker "• "
              - StaticText "Kube-proxy does not perform full-sync operations when operation in large cluster mode (more than 1000 endpoints) ("
              - link "#138635" [ref=e766]
              - StaticText ", "
              - link "@aojea" [ref=e767]
              - StaticText ") [SIG Network]"
            - listitem [level=1]
              - ListMarker "• "
              - StaticText "Kubeadm: during 'kubeadm init', if the default 'admin.conf' and 'super-admin.conf' paths are used, load the files, but construct in memory kubeconfigs that point to the InitConfiguration.localAPIEndpoint instead of the ClusterConfiguration.controlPlaneEndpoint. This would resolve issues with delayed load balancers which are provisioned only after the first kube-apiserver instance starts. ("
              - link "#138682" [ref=e768]
              - StaticText ", "
              - link "@neolit123" [ref=e769]
              - StaticText ") [SIG Cluster Lifecycle]"
            - listitem [level=1]
              - ListMarker "• "
              - StaticText "Kubeadm: skip LocalAPIEndpoint defaulting on 'kubeadm join' for worker nodes. ("
              - link "#138802" [ref=e770]
              - StaticText ", "
              - link "@neolit123" [ref=e771]
              - StaticText ") [SIG Cluster Lifecycle]"
            - listitem [level=1]
              - ListMarker "• "
              - StaticText "Kubeadm: use a dedicated ClusterRole 'system:kubelet-api-admin' for the kube-apiserver kubelet client. ("
              - link "#138958" [ref=e772]
              - StaticText ", "
              - link "@neolit123" [ref=e773]

              - ListMarker "• "
              - StaticText "Kubeadm: when checking the etcd cluster status use a quorum approach, instead of considering the health of all members. This would allow the check to not fail if there are sufficient healthy voting members. ("

              - link "@pohly" [ref=e1294]
              - StaticText ") [SIG API Machinery, Node and Testing]"
            - listitem [level=1]
              - ListMarker "• "
              - StaticText "Fixed a kubelet device manager bug where topology hint computation enumerated O(2^n) NUMA node combinations using all machine NUMA nodes. On systems with many NUMA nodes that carry no devices (e.g. NVIDIA GB200 with 36 NUMA nodes), this caused kubelet to stall indefinitely during pod admission. The device manager now restricts iteration to NUMA nodes that actually host devices for the requested resource, reducing the search space to O(2^k) where k is typically 1–2. ("
              - link "#138244" [ref=e1295]
              - StaticText ", "

            - listitem [level=1]
              - ListMarker "• "
              - StaticText "Previously, when trying to allocate devices through DRA for a node timed out, scheduling would proceed with another node if any had the necessary resources. This potentially hid that a node was ignored. Worse, if scheduling was slow overall, the Pod was incorrectly moved to \"unschedulable\" and only retried after a periodic sweep. Timeouts are now errors that are always visible as Pod scheduling failures and get retried with per-Pod exponential backoff. ("

              - code

              - StaticText " feature gate. Binaries embedding "

          - list
            - listitem [level=1]
              - ListMarker "• "
              - paragraph
                - StaticText "Add ResourcePoolStatusRequest API (v1alpha1) for querying DRA resource pool availability. This enables users and external schedulers to discover available devices across pools before submitting workloads. Requires the DRAResourcePoolStatus feature gate (alpha). ("
                - link "#137028" [ref=e2701]
                - StaticText ", "
                - link "@nmn3m" [ref=e2702]
                - StaticText ") [SIG API Machinery, Apps, Auth, Etcd, Instrumentation, Node, Scheduling, Storage and Testing]"
            - listitem [level=1]
              - ListMarker "• "
              - paragraph
                - StaticText "Added "
                - code
                - StaticText ", "
                - code
                - StaticText " and "
                - code
                - StaticText " fields to Workload and PodGroup APIs to support workload-aware preemption when "
                - code
                - StaticText " feature gate is enabled. ("
                - link "#136589" [ref=e2703]
                - StaticText ", "
                - link "@tosi3k" [ref=e2704]
                - StaticText ") [SIG API Machinery, Apps, Auth, CLI, Cloud Provider, Etcd, Node, Scheduling and Testing]"
            - listitem [level=1]
              - ListMarker "• "
              - paragraph
                - StaticText "DRA (Dynamic Resource Allocation) drivers and controllers now require granular RBAC permissions to update ResourceClaim statuses when the "
                - code
                - StaticText " feature gate is enabled (Beta in 1.36). Schedulers and controllers must be granted "
                - code
                - StaticText "/"
                - code
                - StaticText " on "
                - code
                - StaticText ". DRA drivers must be granted "
                - code
                - StaticText " or "
                - code
                - StaticText " (or patch equivalents) on "
                - code
                - StaticText ", restricted by their specific "
                - code
                - StaticText ". ("
                - link "#134947" [ref=e2705]
                - StaticText ", "
                - link "@aojea" [ref=e2706]
                - StaticText ") [SIG API Machinery, Apps, Auth, Instrumentation, Node, Scheduling and Testing]"
            - listitem [level=1]
              - ListMarker "• "
              - paragraph
                - StaticText "DRA: PodGroup resources can now make requests with ResourceClaims through a "
                - code
                - StaticText " field which can refer to ResourceClaims and ResourceClaimTemplates. Claims made by a PodGroup are reserved for the entire PodGroup instead of individual Pods, allowing more than 256 Pods to share a single ResourceClaim. ResourceClaimTemplates referenced by a PodGroup's claim will replicate into a ResourceClaim specific to that PodGroup able to be shared by all of the group's Pods. ("
                - link "#136989" [ref=e2707]
                - StaticText ", "
                - link "@nojnhuh" [ref=e2708]
                - StaticText ") [SIG API Machinery, Apps, Auth, CLI, Cloud Provider, Etcd, Node, Scheduling and Testing]"
            - listitem [level=1]
              - ListMarker "• "
              - paragraph
                - StaticText "Graduate InPlacePodLevelResourcesVerticalScaling feature to beta and have it on by default. This feature allows resizing the CPU and memory resources at pod-level for pods with pod-level resources set and enabled. ("
                - link "#137684" [ref=e2709]
                - StaticText ", "
                - link "@ndixita" [ref=e2710]
                - StaticText ") [SIG API Machinery, Apps, Autoscaling, Node, Release, Scheduling and Testing]"
            - listitem [level=1]
              - ListMarker "• "
              - paragraph
                - StaticText "Promote NodeLogQuery to GA. ("
                - link "#137544" [ref=e2711]
                - StaticText ", "
                - link "@jrvaldes" [ref=e2712]
                - StaticText ") [SIG Node and Windows]"
            - listitem [level=1]
              - ListMarker "• "
              - paragraph
                - StaticText "[Alpha] Introduce List Types for Attributes in DRA (KEP-5491)."
              - paragraph
                - StaticText "The "
                - code
                - StaticText " feature gate(false by default) can activate below enhancements."
              - paragraph
                - StaticText "For DRA drivers, it can enable list-type fields("
                - code
                - StaticText ") for device attributes in "
                - code
                - StaticText ". Please remember that the number of attribute values, including scalars and lists, per single device is limited to 48."
              - paragraph
                - StaticText "For DRA users, this feature enhances the semantics of "
                - code
                - StaticText "/"
                - code
                - StaticText " constraint in "
                - code
                - StaticText " to work on both scalar and list attributes. The "
                - code
                - StaticText " constraint now matches when the intersection (as a set) of all the list values among candidate devices is non-empty. The "
                - code
                - StaticText " constraint, which is behind the "
                - code
                - StaticText " feature gate, matches when all the list values (as a set) among candidate devices are pairwise disjoint. In both constraints, scalar values are implicitly treated as a singleton set."
              - paragraph
                - StaticText "And, a new CEL function "
                - code
                - StaticText "　is introduced. The function can work on both scalar and list attributes to test inclusion(e.g., "
                - code
                - StaticText "). This can support smooth migration for CEL expression in DRA resources when a DRA driver changes the attribute value type from scalar to list, or vice versa. ("
                - link "#137190" [ref=e2713]
                - StaticText ", "
                - link "@everpeace" [ref=e2714]
                - StaticText ") [SIG API Machinery, Node, Scheduling and Testing]"

              - paragraph
                - StaticText "Adds the ExtendWebSocketsToKubelet feature gate (Beta, default true in v1.36). When enabled, the API server proxies WebSocket exec/attach/portforward requests directly to the kubelet rather than translating or tunneling them at the API server. The kubelet now handles WebSocket-to-SPDY stream translation (exec/attach) and WebSocket tunneling (portforward) using the same handlers previously used at the API server. The kubelet advertises support for this feature to the API server via the NodeDeclaredFeatures mechanism; the API server only proxies directly to a kubelet that has advertised support. Two new ALPHA metrics are added to track routing decisions and WebSocket streaming volume: apiserver_websocket_streaming_requests_total (labels: subresource, proxy_type) and kubelet_streaming_websocket_requests_total (label: subresource). ("

            - listitem [level=1]
              - ListMarker "• "
              - StaticText "K8s.io/client-go/transport now garbage collects TLS cache entries and client certificate rotation go routines when a transport is no longer used. This functionality is enabled by default and can be controlled via the ClientsAllowTLSCacheGC feature gate. Binaries embedding k8s.io/client-go, but not wiring the feature gates can disable it by setting the KUBE_FEATURE_ClientsAllowTLSCacheGC=false environment variable. When the feature is disabled, the TLS cache can grow indefinitely and client certificate rotation go routines are leaked. The new rest_client_transport_cert_rotation_gc_calls_total{} and rest_client_transport_cache_gc_calls_total{result: deleted/skipped} counter metrics can be used with the preexisting rest_client_transport_* metrics to help with debugging.                 - StaticText "Client-go: Informer resync processing improved handling of Resync handling. This reduces contention on store locks between incoming events and handler updates, which may result in observable timing differences of handler invocations. This behavior is guarded by an AtomicFIFO feature gate. This gate is enabled by default in 1.36, but can be disabled if needed to temporarily regain the previous behavior. ("
                - link "#136008" [ref=e3110]
                - link "@michaelasp" [ref=e3111]
            - listitem [level=1]
              - ListMarker "• "
              - paragraph
                - StaticText "Client-go: default informer behavior now updates store state with all the objects in a list or relist, before calling handler OnDelete/OnAdd/OnUpdate methods for individual items which were deleted/added/removed. This ensures that the store state which can be inspected by handlers actually corresponds to a set of objects that existed at a particular resource version on the server. This behavior is guarded by an AtomicFIFO feature gate. This gate is enabled by default in 1.36, but can be disabled if needed to temporarily regain the previous behavior. ("

            - listitem [level=1]
              - ListMarker "• "
              - paragraph
                - StaticText "Client-go: fake client-go (i.e. anything using k8s.io/client-go/testing) now supports separate List+Watch calls with checking of ResourceVersion in the Watch call. This closes a race condition where creating an object directly after an informer cache has synced (= List call completed) and before the Watch call completed would cause that object to not be sent to the informer. A visible side-effect of adding that support is that List meta data contains a ResourceVersion (starting at \"1\" for the empty set, incremented by one for each add/update) and that Watch may return objects where it previously didn't."
